V00001 | -able (affix) | The instrument is portable and can be used in the field.	能够被…；具有…可能性 | 搭配：predictable; identifiable; portable | The instrument is portable and can be used in the field. | 该仪器便携，可用于实地工作。 | 用法：不是所有动词都能自由加 -able；predictable 不等于已被预测。	V00001 U17
V00002 | -ant (affix) | The laboratory replaced the coolant before the next test.	有某种作用的物质或人等 | 搭配：coolant; antidepressant | The laboratory replaced the coolant before the next test. | 实验室在下一次测试前更换了冷却剂。 | 用法：anti-depressant / antidepressant 为药物类别词，不由词缀推断疗效。	V00002 U17
V00003 | -cy (affix) | The team assessed the accuracy of the measurements.	状态；性质 | 搭配：accuracy; urgency | The team assessed the accuracy of the measurements. | 团队评估了测量的准确度。 | 用法：accuracy 是名词，accurate 是形容词。	V00003 U17
V00004 | -ee (affix) | Each trainee completed a supervised task.	受某动作影响的人 | 搭配：employee; trainee | Each trainee completed a supervised task. | 每名受训者完成了一项有监督的任务。 | 用法：并非所有 -ee 词都可机械解释为被动接受者。	V00004 U17
V00005 | -hood (affix) | The study compares experiences in childhood and adulthood.	状态；阶段 | 搭配：childhood; adulthood; likelihood | The study compares experiences in childhood and adulthood. | 该研究比较童年与成年经历。 | 用法：likelihood 的形容词相关形式是 likely；注意拼写。	V00005 U17
V00006 | -ify (affix) | The appendix clarifies the coding procedure.	使具有某种性质 | 搭配：clarify; purify; unify; beautify | The appendix clarifies the coding procedure. | 附录澄清了编码程序。 | 用法：clarification / purification / unification 为相应名词。	V00006 U17
V00007 | -ise / -ize (affix) | The department publicised the symposium on its website.	使处于某状态 | 搭配：modernise; modernize; colonise; colonize; publicise; publicize | The department publicised the symposium on its website. | 该系在网站上宣传了研讨会。 | 用法：英美拼写及出版社偏好不同；全文保持一致。	V00007 U17
V00008 | -ism / -ist (affix) | The seminar examines modernism in literature.	思想、学说、体系，或行为、性质／持某观点、态度或从事某活动的人 | 搭配：heroism; modernism; anarchist; optimist; Marxism; extremist | The seminar examines modernism in literature. | 研讨课研究文学中的现代主义。 | 用法：modernist 可为名词或形容词；Marxism 大写。	V00008 U17
V00009 | -less (affix) | Without a clear definition, the label is meaningless.	没有…的 | 搭配：meaningless; colourless; fearless | Without a clear definition, the label is meaningless. | 没有明确的定义，这个标签没有意义。 | 用法：词义由语境限定；colourless 的美式拼写为 colorless。	V00009 U17
V00010 | -ocracy / -ocrat (affix) | The course compares democracy and autocracy.	政体／与某种政体有关的统治者或支持者 | 搭配：autocracy; autocrat; democracy; democrat | The course compares democracy and autocracy. | 该课程比较民主政体与专制政体。 | 用法：autocrat指独裁统治者；democrat可指民主制度支持者。democratic、democratise/democratize属于相关词族。	V00010 U17
V00011 | -proof (affix) | The field instrument requires a dustproof case.	防…；耐… | 搭配：waterproof; soundproof; dustproof | The field instrument requires a dustproof case. | 该野外仪器需要防尘外壳。 | 用法：某些构词表示抗性；实际性能仍取决于规范与试验。	V00011 U17
V00012 | a contradiction in terms (n. phrase) | In ordinary Euclidean geometry, a circular square is a contradiction in terms.	用词本身自相矛盾 | 搭配：a contradiction in terms | In ordinary Euclidean geometry, a circular square is a contradiction in terms. | 在通常的欧氏几何中，圆形正方形在用词上自相矛盾。 | 用法：这里按圆和正方形的普通几何定义判断；不同于两个观测结果不一致。	V00012 U44
